## Intended complete scope

Product Version classified ID set and optional primary member, safe closed parser,
current same-Brand Category lifecycle/policy validation through public Category
source in owning transaction; actual Draft create/replace and immutable operation
replay/snapshot persistence; current source/Event generation invalidation; Product
filter/count consumers bound to actual classifications. Migration forward-only in
existing1100 Catalog namespace, no legacy history rewrite or inference that old
absent classifications were an explicitly empty set. New commands may explicitly
create empty set; legacy records preserve unknown coverage until owner migration
or approved evidence. No inferred Product classifications from Menu structure.

Current Actor/Tenant association/field/Phase holders stay mandatory and unconfigured
providers fail closed. Draft/Active Category acceptance depends on current owning
policy, not caller assertions. No publishing, tax/price/Inventory/Supplier/Provider
business changes or invented lifecycle policy. Normal UI/source assembly is not
production, Store/UAT or complete-project evidence. Commit/push/merge/deploy/live
DDL/roles/services remain unauthorized.

## Acceptance and verification selection

1. Combined prior Product/Category public source, Event contracts/manifests/owner
   rules preserve both lineages; current migration catalog contains202canonical files.
2. Category references form a closed bounded unique ID set; primary is null or
   included member; malformed/accessor/foreign input fails safely. Legacy absence
   remains explicit absence, no default classification attached to old snapshots.
3. Product-owned expected-version/idempotent Draft mutations validate actual held
   Category facts and commit Product/Audit/Event/source together. Replay returns
   original immutable classified result after subsequent changes.
4. Real PostgreSQL forced RLS, same-Brand/lifecycle/current policy, authority through
   COMMIT, concurrent Category change, rollback/lost-ack and complete source proof.
5. Product category filters and Category counts consume public actual classification
   evidence and fail unavailable for incomplete legacy coverage.
